Main optimizer functions:

- `grad_from_weights(weights, a, A, N)`: Q=fft(weights,N); g=2*real(ifft(Q*np.conj(A)))[:n]; return g.
- `lse_value(c,cmax,beta)`: if beta small, cmax; else cmax + log(sum exp(beta(c-cmax)))/beta.

Adam optimizer:
```
def adam_lse(a, n,S,N, time, rng, beta_start, beta_end, lr, momentum=0.9, max_iter?...)
  a = project(a,S)
  c,A,...; cmax
  best...
  m = zeros; v=zeros; t=1
  beta = beta_start
  lr = base/(n? S?)
  while time:
    g, lse = lse_grad(a,N,A,beta)
    t+=1
    bias...
    step = lr / (sqrt(v)+1e-8)
    cand = a - step*grad  # maybe project
    # optional reduce step if cmax worsens too much? Adam no line search; we can compute cmax of cand? We need to update a to cand, then next loop compute c. To track best, compute conv of cand each iteration anyway? The next loop will compute, but for best we can compute cmax with conv after project. That's one conv. But gradient needed next; we can combine: cand_project, compute c,A for cand and use as next? We can restructure loop to compute gradient after update, avoiding double.
```
Pseudo:
Initialize a, compute c,A, beta.
Loop:
  weights, lse, cmax from c.
  grad = ... using A.
  adam update a_new.
  compute c_new,A_new for a_new; cmax_new.
  if cmax_new < best: store.
  a,c,A = a_new,c_new,A_new.
  adapt beta, lr.
  periodic spectral restart.
This uses per iteration: one fft(q), one ifft(grad), one fft(a_new), one ifft(a_new*a_new). That's 4 transforms. Good.

Upsample function details:
```
def upsample_seq(p, n_target):
    m=len(p)
    if m==n_target: return p.copy()
    p=sum normalized to 1
    # coordinate mapping: density on [0,m) samples p_i, target [0,n)
    x = np.arange(n) * (m / n)
    j = np.floor(x).astype(int)
    frac = x - j
    # clamp j to m-2 if m>1? if j>=m, j=m-1, frac=0
    j2 = np.minimum(j+1, m-1)
    val = (1-frac)*p[j] + frac*p[j2]
    # scale because ∑ p(x) dx ≈ m * mean p? We want ∑ val? Actually val approximates density p(x); p_i are probabilities, density ≈ m p_i. ∑_{target} (m/n)*val ≈1. So q=(m/n)*val.
    q = (m/n)*val
    q=np.maximum(q,0); q=q/q.sum()
    return q
```

cmax=c.max()
b=float(beta)
if b < 1e-5: weights = np.ones_like(c)/len(c); val=cmax
else:
  z=b*(c-cmax)
  # z <=0; max 0. exp safe. sum.
  ez=np.exp(z)
  tot=ez.sum()
  if tot < 1e-300: idx=np.argmax(c); weights zeros; weights[idx]=1; val=cmax
  else: weights=ez/tot; val=cmax + np.log(tot)/b
Q=fft(weights,N)
g=2.0 * np.real(ifft(Q * np.conj(A)))[:n]
return g,val
```

Spectral restart partial:
```
def spectral_partial(a,N,n,rng,frac):
  A=fft(a,N)
  mag=np.abs(A); phase=np.angle(A)
  num=int(N*frac); idx=rng.choice(N, num, replace=False)
  phase[idx]=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,num)
  a_new=np.real(ifft(mag*np.exp(1j*phase)))[:n]
  return project(a_new,S?) # need S passed
```

`optimize_adam` implementation more concrete:
```
def optimize_adam(a0, n, S, N, duration, rng):
    a=project_simplex(a0,S)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n)
    cmax=c.max()
    best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax
    beta=3.0
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    t=0
    step_base = 0.25 * S / n  # 0.0028 for 16384
    last_best=time
    no_improve_iters=0
    last_stdall=0
    start=time
    while time<deadline-start? We'll pass deadline absolute.
       if time>=deadline: break
       t+=1; beta=min(200,beta*1.002) perhaps
       g,_=lse_grad(...)
       b1=0.9; b2=0.999
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
       vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       step = step_base * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       # limit step L2?
       sn=np.linalg.norm(step)
       max_step = 0.02 # mass? S? Let's use max_step = 0.05 * np.sqrt(np.mean(a**2)) * np.sqrt(n)? Hmm.
       # Simpler: if sn > step_base*10, scale? Not useful.
       cand=project_simplex(a-step,S)
       cnew,Anew=compute_conv(cand,N,n); cm=cnew.max()
       if cm > cmax + 1e-8:
           # try half
           cand2=project_simplex(a-0.5*step,S)
           c2,A2=compute_conv(cand2,N,n); cm2=c2.max()
           if cm2 <= cm:
               cand=cand2; cnew=c2; Anew=A2; cm=cm2
           # if still worse, maybe accept? Maybe skip update if worse
           if cm > cmax + max(1e-8,0.01*cmax):
               # revert and adjust step_base smaller
               step_base *= 0.8
               continue
       a=cand; c=cnew; A=Anew; cmax=cm
       if cmax < best_c - 1e-9:
           best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax; last_best=time; no_improve=0; step_base=min(0.5*S/n, step_base*1.2)
       else:
           no_improve=time-last_best
           if no_improve>30:
               step_base *=0.7; no_improve=0
               if t % 1000 ==0:
                   a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,0.1); c,A=...; m*=0.5; v*=0.5
    return best,best_c
```
Potential: LSE gradient at beta increases, weight concentrates; step_base independent. Good.

But `mhat` can be large early due v small; first step maybe step_base* huge? Since m and v same scale, ratio ~1. If g has zero components, okay. Step per coord ~step_base. For n=16384, 0.0028; total L2 ~0.0028*√n=0.35, large! Project to simplex may drastically change. Too large? a_mean 0.011; step 0.0028 per coord, 25% of mean. Sum changes? Project handles. Could be too aggressive. Set step_base=0.05*S/n=0.00055, L2 0.011; 5% typical. Use `lr_scale=0.1`, step_base=0.1*S/n=0.0011, L2 0.014. Good. We'll make `step_base = 0.1 * S / n`.

`max_step` optional: if有很多 coordinates step large due denominator small, but mhat/vhat ratio bounded for nonzero g? If g components vary, some small v -> step >stepbase. Could be large. Limit per-coordinate or vector: `step = np.clip(step, -5*step_base, 5*step_base)`? Since gradient sign direction, clipping may help. Use `np.maximum(-10*step_base, np.minimum(10*step_base, step))`. Or L2 limit. We'll do vector L2 limit: `max_norm = 0.05 * S`? S ~181 ->9 huge. `max_norm = 0.05 * np.linalg.norm(a)`. norm(a) ~? sum S, uniform norm ~S/√2? For uniform, norm²=n*(S/n)²=S²/n=2, norm√2=1.414. 5% =0.07. Step L2 around0.014, below. Good! Let's set `max_norm = 0.1 * np.linalg.norm(a)` (0.14). If step_norm > max_norm, scale. Good.

In L-BFGS, similar step unit? We won't use L2 limit? Could.

Let's set: `direction = lbfgs_dir(...); dn=np.linalg.norm(direction); if dn>0: direction=direction/dn; step=0.05*np.linalg.norm(a)`? That is L2 step 0.07, per coord ~0.0005, okay. With line search. Coarse search routine:
```
def coarse_search(budget, rng, n_full?) -> list of coarse best (p arrays length m, score)
  m=1024; S_c=sqrt(2*m); N_c=nextpow2(2*m-1)
  inits=[]
  add uniform, u_shape, beta, random maybe, parent downsample.
  total per init = budget/len(inits) *0.8
  for each:
     a=project(init*S_c,S_c)
     a,c = optimize_adam(a,..., per_init*0.4, rng, beta_start=3,betamax=100)
     a,c = optimize_lbfgs(a,..., per_init*0.6, rng, beta_start=20,betamax=300)
     update best list if score improvement, dedupe: compare coarse score; keep top 3.
```

def make_u_shape(n, gamma=1.0):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    w=1.0 + gamma*(2*x-1)**2
    return w
```

Main propose timeline pseudocode:
```
def propose_candidate():
  budget=2400.0
  t0=time; deadline=t0+max(0,budget-25)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
  n=16384
  S=sqrt(2*n); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  best_a=None; best_score=inf
  # Initial from parent
  parent_found=False
  try:
    par=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float).ravel()
    if par.size>=256:
       par=np.clip(par,0,None); if sum>0: par=par/par.sum(); parent_found=True
  except: pass
  if parent_found and len(par)<=n*2? upsample to n and normalize.
     a=upsample_seq(par,n)*S
     score=sc(cmax)
     update
  else:
     a=uniform*S; score...
  # If best_score is inf fallback uniform.
  # Phase A: Adam parent
  timeA = min(0.15*duration, deadline-now-20)
  if timeA>30:
    a_res,sc=optimize_adam(best_a,n,S,N,timeA,rng,100)
    update
  # Phase B: L-BFGS parent
  timeB = min(0.25*duration,...)
  if timeB>60:
    a_res,sc=optimize_lbfgs(best_a,...,timeB,rng,beta_start=10,betamax=500)
    update
  # Phase C: coarse
  timeC = min(0.12*duration, ...)
  top_coarse=[]
  if timeC>120:
     top_coarse=coarse_search(timeC, rng, m=512 or 1024, parent if exists)
  # Phase D: full coarse top 2
  timeD = min(0.22*duration,...)
  for cseq in top_coarse[:2]: allocate timeD/len; full_upsample; optimize_adam 40%, lbfgs 60%
  # Phase E: final polish best: alternate Adam and L-BFGS in 60s slices until deadline-20
  while time left>100:
     # choose Adam if best stagnant? just alternate: Adam 100s then L-BFGS 200s
  # Final shift/sym
  if time left>30: best_a=improve_shifts(best_a,..., time_limit)
  # Postprocess: clip, project final? But shifting preserves sum; if we sym/project after shift maybe scale. Ensure final sum S.
  best_a=clip(project_simplex(best_a,S),0,1000)
  return list(map(float,best_a))
```

Pseudo detailed `optimize_lbfgs`:
```
def optimize_lbfgs(a0,n,S,N,deadline,rng,beta_start=10,beta_end=500,m_hist=12):
    a=project_simplex(np.asarray(a0,float),S)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    beta=beta_start
    step=0.05*float(np.linalg.norm(a)) + 1e-12
    it=0
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    no_improve=0.0
    # compute initial g
    g,_=lse_gradient(a,N,n,c,A,beta)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       now=time.monotonic()
       if it % 300 ==0:
           beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.12)
       if now - last_best > 120 and it % 200 ==0:
           # stall: spectral restart
           frac=0.1 + min(0.2, 0.02*it? no)
           a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,frac=0.15)
           c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n); cmax=c.max()
           s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
           beta=max(beta_start, beta*0.7)
           step=0.05*norm(a)
           g,_=lse_gradient(...)
           last_best=now
           continue
       # compute direction using current g
       direction=lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g,n)
       dn=norm(direction)
       if dn<1e-14: break
       direction /= dn
       a_old=a.copy()
       c_old=c; cmax_old=cmax
       # evaluate candidates [step,0.5,2]
       candidates=[]
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
           cand=project_simplex(a_old + mult*step*direction, S)
           # maybe also negative direction? L-BFGS should descend; sometimes direction ascent fallback.
           c_ls,A_ls=compute_conv(cand,N,n)
           cm=float(c_ls.max())
           candidates.append((cm,cand,c_ls,A_ls,mult))
       # pick best if <= cmax_old + 1e-10
       candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # no lambda! Need avoid lambda. Use key function? We can just loop manually find min.
```
No lambda. We'll find min_index manually.
```
       best_idx=0
       for idx in range(1,len(candidates)):
          if candidates[idx][0] < candidates[best_idx][0]: best_idx=idx
       cm_best,cand_best,c_best,A_best,mult_best = candidates[best_idx]
       if cm_best <= cmax_old + 1e-10:
           # accept
           a=cand_best; c=c_best; A=A_best; cmax=cm_best
           if cmax < best_c - 1e-8:
               best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax; last_best=time.monotonic()
               step = min(0.1*norm(a), step*1.1)
           # adapt step: if mult 2.0 increase; 0.5 decrease
           if mult_best >=2.0: step=min(0.1*norm(a), step*1.2)
           elif mult_best <=0.5: step=max(1e-8*S/n, step*0.7)
           # update history
           g_new,_=lse_gradient(a,N,n,c,A,beta)
           s_k=a-a_old
           y_k=g_new-g
           sy=np.dot(s_k,y_k); ss=np.dot(s_k,s_k)
           if sy > 1e-12*ss and ss > 1e-20:
               s_hist.append(s_k); y_hist.append(y_k)
               if len(s_hist)>m_hist: s_hist=s_hist[-m_hist:]; y_hist=y_hist[-m_hist:]
           g=g_new
       else:
           # no improve: perturb direction? reduce step, maybe random kick
           step *= 0.5
           # If step very small, spectral restart
           if step < 1e-5*S/n:
               a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,0.1)
               c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n); cmax=c.max()
               s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
               g,_=lse_gradient(...)
           continue
    return best,best_c
```
Need avoid copy a_old each iteration? okay. Candidate list stores arrays; memory 3 arrays * 16k minimal. Sort manual.

But using same A? For candidates, c_ls,A_ls. If accept one, use its A. Good.

`lbfgs_dir`: It needs n for zero. We'll pass n. The direction from L-BFGS maybe not a descent direction if history bad; we check dot(g,direction) and fallback -g. Then normalize. Good.

`lse_gradient`: We call it after accept to compute g_new. Then next loop uses. At start, compute. Good.

Potential inefficiency: We compute gradient at start of each loop, then after accept compute new gradient. So two gradients per accepted outer? Actually after accept, set g=g_new; next loop will not recompute? In code, at top of loop we may compute g? Need structure:
- Before while, compute g.
- Inside while, use g for direction. After accept, compute g_new and assign g. Then loop continues, does not compute g at top. So one gradient per accepted iteration plus candidates. If no accept, g still old; we may recompute if restart. Good. Thus per accepted: candidate convs (3*2=6 transforms) + grad_new (2) =8. Good. For no accept: 6 transforms. Good.

At stall, recompute g after restart.

Need be careful `g` vector may be NaN if weights all zero? lse handles.

Adam implementation can be similar:
```
def optimize_adam(...):
  a=project
  c,A; cmax; best
  beta=beta_start
  m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0
  step_base=0.08*S/n
  last_best=...
  while time<deadline:
    now
    if now-last_best>60:
       if m count>0: step_base*=0.7; m*=0.5; v*=0.5
       if it%500==0: a=spectral_partial(0.05)
    beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.003)
    g,_=lse_gradient(...)
    t+=1
    m=0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.999*v+0.001*g*g
    mhat=m/(1-0.9**t)
    vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
    step=step_base*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    # Clip to max_percoord = 5*step_base maybe? Could distort; use L2:
    sn=norm(step); max_norm=0.05*norm(a); if sn>max_norm: step *= max_norm/sn
    cand=project(a-step,S)
    c_new,A_new=compute_conv(cand,N,n); cm=new.max()
    if cm > cmax + 1e-10:
       cand2=project(a-0.5*step,S); ... if cm2<cm accept else # if still worse, maybe don't update? Let's accept if worsening <=20%; else revert.
       if still worse by >0.05*cmax: step_base*=0.7; continue
    a=cand; c=c_new; A=A_new; cmax=cm
    if cmax<best_c: best; last_best; step_base=min(0.2*S/n, step_base*1.1)
    # periodically reduce beta? no
  return best,best_c
```
— Adam can better than lbfgs? We need tune. Maybe accept small worsening to escape? For basin, not. Could use annealing: if `rng.random()<0.05` and cm < cmax*(1+1/(1+it/1000)) accept. Might help but risk. Keep deterministic.

LSE beta schedule in Adam: gradually high. At high beta, gradient top peak. Step_base independent.

def sort? no.

Dedup coarse:
```
def is_distinct(seq, candidates, thr=0.5):
   for other in candidates:
       # compare correlation or L2 normalized
       x=(seq-seq.mean()); y=(other-other.mean())
       norm = sqrt(dot(x,x)*dot(y,y)); if norm<1e-12: corr=0 else corr=dot(x,y)/norm
       if abs(corr)>0.95 or abs(np.mean(seq-np.mean(other)) < 1e-3): return False
   return True
```

def improve_transforms(a,n,S,N,deadline,rng):

Spectral restart:
```
def spectral_partial(a, N, n, S, rng, frac):
   A=fft(a,N)
   mag=np.abs(A)
   phase=np.angle(A)
   num=int(N*frac)
   if num<=0: return a.copy()
   idx=rng.choice(N, size=num, replace=False)
   phase[idx] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi, num)
   an=np.real(ifft(mag*np.exp(1j*phase)))[:n]
   an=np.maximum(an,0)
   return project_simplex(an,S)
```
If frac>=0.99, full random. Use num int; if N large, rng.choice O(num) okay. Partial 0.1 * 32768=3277 choice maybe ok. For frequent restart, use smaller frac 0.05.

def make_u_shape(n, gamma):

   w=1.0 + gamma*(2*x-1)*(2*x-1)
   return np.maximum(w,1e-6)
```

m=len(p)
if m==target: return p.copy()
if m<1: return ones(target)
p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return ones(target)
p=p/s
x=np.arange(target, dtype=float) * (float(m)/float(target))
j=np.floor(x).astype(int)
with np.errstate(divide='ignore',invalid='ignore'): frac=x-j
j=np.minimum(j, m-1)
j2=np.minimum(j+1, m-1)
val=(1-frac)*p[j] + frac*p[j2]
val = val * (float(m)/float(target))
val=np.maximum(val,0)
s=val.sum()
if s<=0: return np.ones(target)/target
return val/s
```

Coarse search code:
```
def coarse_search(budget,start_time,rng,parent_seq):
   m=1024
   S_c=sqrt(2*m); N_c=next_pow2(2*m-1)
   inits=...
   total=len(inits)
   overall_deadline=start_time+budget
   cand=[]
   for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= overall_deadline - 10: break
       # reserve for remaining inits? per_rem = 0.8*(overall_deadline-now)/(total-idx)
       per_time = min(120.0, 0.75*(overall_deadline - now) / max(1,total-idx))
       block_deadline = now + per_time
       a = project(init*S_c,S_c)
       a, sc = polish_candidate(a, m, S_c, N_c, block_deadline, rng)
       # compute final score
       add candidate if sc < inf and (not recent)
   # sort top3 manually
   return top
```

`improve_transforms` code:
```
def improve_by_shifts(a,n,S,N,deadline,rng):
    a=project_simplex(a,S)
    best=a.copy(); best_c=conv_max(a,n,N)
    # sym
    b=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); b=project_simplex(b,S); sc=conv_max... update
    # deterministic shifts
    for s in [n//4,n//2,3*n//4]: b=np.roll(best,s); sc=... update
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       if s==0: continue
       b=np.roll(best,s)
       sc=conv_max(b,n,N)
       if sc < best_c - 1e-12:
           best=b.copy(); best_c=sc
    return best,best_c
```
Conv_max with b length n. This uses no LSE. It can be expensive if deadline large. We'll set final transform deadline = deadline - 5, but maybe limit to 30s. Use `trans_deadline = min(deadline-5, time.monotonic()+40.0)`? Better: in main, before final transform, `if deadline-now > 40: improve with deadline=min(deadline-5, now+40)`. Manual sort function:
```
def sort_candidates_cands(cands):
    for i in range(len(cands)-1):
        for j in range(len(cands)-1, i, -1):
            if cands[j][1] < cands[j-1][1]:
                cands[i], cands[j] = cands[j], cands[i-1]? Need careful. Simple:
```
Use selection:
```
def sort_cands_small(cands):
    # sort ascending by score (index1)
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        min_idx=i
        for j in range(i+1,len(cands)):
            if cands[j][1] < cands[min_idx][1]: min_idx=j
        if min_idx != i:
            tmp=cands[i]; cands[i]=cands[min_idx]; cands[min_idx]=tmp
    return cands
```
Works.

Add candidate with dedup:
```
def add_coarse(cands,arr,score,maxc=3):
   if score >= inf: return cands
   x=arr - arr.mean()
   nx=np.linalg.norm(x)
   for existing in cands:
       y=existing[0]-existing[0].mean(); ny=np.linalg.norm(y)
       if nx>0 and ny>0:
          corr=abs(np.dot(x,y)/(nx*ny))
          if corr > 0.98: return cands
   cands.append([arr, score])
   sort_cands_small(cands)
   if len(cands) > maxc: del cands[-1]
   return cands
```
Works. For coarse lengths 1024, computing norms cheap.

`polish_candidate` top-level:
```
def polish_candidate(a,n,S,N,deadline,rng):
    a=project_simplex(np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1),S)
    cmax=conv_max(a,n,N)
    best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax
    if a.size != n: ... # not needed
    if deadline-now > 25:
       d=min(0.35*(deadline-now), deadline-now-5)
       a2,s2=optimize_adam(a,n,S,N,now+d,rng,3,200)
       if s2<best_c: best=a2.copy(); best_c=s2
    if deadline-now > 40:
       d=min(0.65*(deadline-now), deadline-now-5)
       a3,s3=optimize_lbfgs(best,n,S,N,now+d,rng,10,800)
       if s3<best_c: best=a3.copy(); best_c=s3
    best=project_simplex(best,S)
    return best,best_c
```

Pseudo code for lbfgs:
```
def optimize_lbfgs(a0,n,S,N,deadline,rng,beta_start=10.0,beta_end=800.0,m_hist=12):
    a=project_simplex(a0,S)
    c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax
    beta=float(beta_start)
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    norm_a=float(np.linalg.norm(a))
    step=0.05*norm_a if norm_a>0 else 0.01*S/n
    g,_=lse_gradient(a,N,n,c,A,beta)
    it=0
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it +=1
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline: break
        if it % 250 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.12)
        if now - last_best > 180.0:
            if cmax > best_c + 0.02:
                a=best.copy()
            else:
                frac = 0.15 if it < 2000 else 0.25
                a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,frac)
            c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n)
            cmax=float(c.max())
            s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
            beta = max(float(beta_start), beta*0.75)
            norm_a=float(np.linalg.norm(a))
            step=0.05*norm_a if norm_a>0 else 0.01*S/n
            g,_=lse_gradient(a,N,n,c,A,beta)
            last_best=now
            continue
        direction=lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g,n)
        dn=float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
        if dn < 1e-14:
            break
        direction = direction / dn
        a_old=a.copy(); c_old=c; cmax_old=cmax
        mults=(1.0,0.5,2.0)
        # candidate 1
        cand = mults[0]*step
        a1=project_simplex(a_old + cand*direction,S)
        c1,A1=compute_conv(a1,N,n); cm1=float(c1.max())
        # cand2
        a2=project_simplex(a_old + 0.5*step*direction,S)
        c2,A2=compute_conv(a2,N,n); cm2=float(c2.max())
        # cand3
        a3=project_simplex(a_old + 2.0*step*direction,S)
        c3,A3=compute_conv(a3,N,n); cm3=float(c3.max())
        # choose best among 1,2,3
        best_idx=1; best_cm=cm1; best_mult=1.0
        if cm2 < best_cm: best_idx=2; ...
        if cm3 < best_cm: best_idx=3; ...
        if best_cm <= cmax_old + 1e-9:
            if best_idx==1: a_new=a1; c_new=c1; A_new=A1; best_mult=1.0
            elif ...
            else accept;
            cmax=best_cm
            if cmax < best_c - 1e-10:
                best=a_new.copy(); best_c=cmax; last_best=time.monotonic()
            # adapt step
            norm_new=float(np.linalg.norm(a_new))
            if best_mult >= 1.5:
                step = min(0.10*norm_new, step*1.2)
            elif best_mult <=0.5:
                step = max(1e-8*S/n, step*0.7)
            else:
                step = min(0.10*norm_new, max(1e-8*S/n, step*1.05))
            g_new,_=lse_gradient(a_new,N,n,c_new,A_new,beta)
            s_k=a_new-a_old
            y_k=g_new-g
            sy=float(np.dot(s_k,y_k)); ss=float(np.dot(s_k,s_k))
            if sy > 1e-12*ss and ss > 1e-20:
                s_hist.append(s_k); y_hist.append(y_k)
                if len(s_hist)>m_hist: s_hist=s_hist[-m_hist:]; y_hist=y_hist[-m_hist:]
            a=a_new; c=c_new; A=A_new; g=g_new
        else:
            # no improvement; shrink and maybe kick
            step=max(1e-8*S/n, step*0.6)
            # if step extremely small, small random perturbation
            if step < 1e-5*S/n:
                a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,0.05)
                c,A=compute_conv(a,N,n)
                cmax=float(c.max())
                s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
                beta=max(float(beta_start), beta*0.8)
                g,_=lse_gradient(a,N,n,c,A,beta)
    return best,best_c
```
Line search c_old and cmax_old: If best_cm is from candidate 2, c_new etc. Good.

Potential memory: a1,a2,a3 and c1,c2,c3 and A1,A2,A3 all in scope until next iter; okay. Could delete after loop.

Adam implementation details:
```
def optimize_adam(a0,...):
 a=project; c,A; cmax; best
 beta=float(beta_start); b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
 m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
 norm_a=norm; step_base=0.1*S/n
 last_best=now
 it=0
 while time<deadline:
   it+=1
   now=...
   if it % 100 ==0: beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.2)
   if now-last_best > 60:
      step_base=max(1e-6*S/n, step_base*0.7)
      if it % 2000 ==0:
         a=spectral_partial(a,N,n,S,rng,0.05); c,A=...; cmax=...; m*=0.5; v*=0.5
         last_best=now
   g,_=lse_gradient(...)
   t +=1
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
   mhat = m/(1-b1**t)
   vhat = v/(1-b2**t)
   step = step_base * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
   # L2 cap
   snorm=norm(step); cap=0.05*norm(a) maybe if norm(a)>0 else norm_a
   if snorm>cap>0: step*=cap/snorm
   cand=project(a-step,S)
   c_new,A_new=compute_conv(cand,N,n); cm=...
   if not finite: cm=inf
   if cm > cmax + 1e-9:
       cand2=project(a-0.5*step,S); c2,A2=...; cm2=...
       if cm2 < cm: cand=cand2; c_new=c2; A_new=A2; cm=cm2
       if cm > cmax + max(1e-8, 0.02*cmax):
           step_base=max(1e-7*S/n, step_base*0.7)
           continue
   a=cand; c=c_new; A=A_new; cmax=cm
   if cmax < best_c -1e-9:
      best=a.copy(); best_c=cmax; last_best=now; step_base=min(0.3*S/n, step_base*1.15)
   elif now-last_best > 120:
      # if degrading, reset to best?
      if cmax > best_c + 0.05:
          a=best.copy(); c,A=compute_conv(a); cmax=best_c; m*=0.5; v*=0.5
   # ensure if no finite? break if cmax inf
 if cmax inf? return best
```
`b1**t` may underflow for t large? b1**10000 ~ 2e-47 okay; for t 1e6 maybe 0 but we don't run that many. Could use running bias without powers? Fine. For t maybe 10000, 0.9**10000 ~ 1.7e-46 > 0; 1 - that =1. good. For b2 .999**10000 ~ 0.0043. okay.

`step_base* mhat/(sqrtv)`: if g zero, step zero, candidate same; cmax no change; stall. Will reset. Good.

Should `beta_end` for Adam in `polish_candidate` be 500? Could set 500. For handling high peak, yes. If beta too high, gradient one-hot at max; Adam may focus. Good.

We can compute. Use `if cmax < 2.0: beta_start=max(20, 10*(cmax/1.5))`? Simpler pass constants. For full initial parent score ~1.506; lbfgs beta_start=20 okay. For coarse random maybe score high; beta_start=10. Good.

def lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g,n):
   m=len(s_hist)
   gn=norm(g)
   if m==0 or gn<1e-18:
      if gn<1e-18: return np.zeros(n)
      return -g/gn
   q=g.copy(); alphas=zeros(m)
   ...
   direction=-r
   if dot(g,direction)>0: direction=-g
   return direction
```

Shapes:
```
def make_uniform(n): return np.ones(n)
def make_u_shape(n,gamma): x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); t=2*x-1; return np.maximum(1.0+gamma*t*t,1e-6)
def make_beta_shape(n,a): i=np.arange(1.0,n+1.0); w=np.power(i,a)*np.power(n+1-i,a); return np.maximum(w,1e-6)
def make_cos_shape(n,freq,amp,ph): x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); w=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph); return np.maximum(w,1e-6)
def make_random_uniform(n,rng): return rng.random(n)+1e-3
def make_spectral_random(n,N,rng,sigma=0.4): ... if sum<=0 return make_random
```

```
def coarse_search(budget,start_time,rng,parent_seq):
  m=1024
  S_c=np.sqrt(2.0*float(m)); N_c=next_pow2(2*m-1)
  overall_deadline=start_time+budget
  inits=[]
  inits.append(('u', make_uniform(m)))
  ...
  if parent_seq is not None:
      try: par=np.asarray(parent_seq,float).ravel(); if finite and sum>0: inits.append(('par', resize_seq(par,m)))
      except: pass
  cands=[]
  now=start_time
  total_inits=len(inits)
  for idx in range(total_inits):
      now=time.monotonic()
      if now >= overall_deadline - 5: break
      rem_for_rest = overall_deadline - now
      per = 0.85 * rem_for_rest / max(1, total_inits-idx)
      per = min(per, 150.0)
      block_deadline = now + per
      arr = inits[idx][1]
      a = project_simplex(arr*S_c, S_c)
      a, sc = polish_candidate(a,m,S_c,N_c,block_deadline,rng,3,200,10,500)
      # if sc close? add
      cands=add_coarse_candidate(cands, a, sc, 3)
  sort_cands_small(cands)
  return cands
```

def propose_candidate():
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=2400.0
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,budget-30.0)
    total_budget=deadline-t0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123456)
    n_full=16384
    S_full=...
    N_full=...
    parent_np=None
    try: ...

           S_native=sqrt; N_native=...

           native_score=conv_max
    # full

       full_base=resize_seq(parent_np,n_full)
    else:
       full_base=np.ones(n_full)
    full_a=project(full_base*S_full,S_full); full_score=conv_max
    if full_a is None? no.
    print scores

       dur=min(0.10*total_budget, max(0.0, deadline-now-300))

          deadline_phase=now+dur

          if s_new<native_score: ...
          print
          # update full from native if good enough

              fa=resize_seq(native_a,n_full)*S_full; fa=project(fa,S_full); fs=conv_max
              if fs<full_score: full_a,full_score=fa,fs

       if s_new<full_score: full_a=...

             arr=top[i][0]
             fa=resize_seq(arr,n_full); fa=project(fa*S_full,S_full)
             fs_initial=conv_max(fa,...)

             if deadline-now > 60:
                block_deadline=min(now+per, deadline-50)
                a_new,s_new=polish_candidate(fa,n_full,S_full,N_full,block_deadline,rng,5,400,20,1000)
                if s_new<full_score: full_a=...
                print
    # final adaptive loop
    final_done=False
    while not final_done:
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=deadline-now
       if rem < 200: break
       if native_a is not None and native_score <= full_score:
           work_native=True
       else: work_native=False
       dur=min(120.0, 0.25*total_budget, rem-100)

          if s_new<full_score: ...
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-slice_start
       if elapsed < 5.0:

              polish native

```

```python
"""
Two-scale search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / sum(a)^2.

Key ingredients:
- Coarse-to-fine: optimize small-length sequences, then upsample and refine.
- Exact FFT-based gradient for a log-sum-exp surrogate of max(a*a).
- Adam + L-BFGS polishing on the probability simplex.
- Spectral-phase restarts and final shift/symmetrization post-optimization.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_candidates = (cumsum - float(S)) / idx
    valid = theta_candidates <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
    theta = (cumsum[rho - 1] - float(S)) / float(rho)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = out.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, float(S) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return out * (float(S) / s)


def compute_conv(a, N, n):
    """Full linear convolution a*a by FFT, clipped to non-negative."""
    A = fft(a, n=N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, A


def conv_max(a, n, N):
    """Return max (a*a) for length n, assuming a is already normalized."""
    A = fft(a, n=N)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[: 2 * n - 1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float("inf")
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(c.max())


def lse_gradient(a, N, n, c, A, beta):
    """
    Gradient of LSE(beta, max(c)) for c = a*a on the simplex.
    Returns centered gradient and smoothed value.
    """
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    beta = float(beta)

    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    if beta < 1e-6:
        weights = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        val = cmax
    else:
        z = beta * (c - cmax)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        tot = float(ez.sum())
        if tot < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(tot):
            weights = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            weights[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            val = cmax
        else:
            weights = ez / tot
            val = cmax + float(np.log(tot)) / beta

    Q = fft(weights, n=N)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(Q * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    # Remove the constant component: the simplex constraint makes it irrelevant.
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    return g, val


def lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g, n):
    """Two-loop L-BFGS recursion."""
    m = len(s_hist)
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if m == 0 or gn < 1e-18:
        if gn < 1e-18:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return -g / gn

    q = np.array(g, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    alphas = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            alphas[i] = 0.0
        else:
            alphas[i] = float(np.dot(s_i, q)) / yy
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_i

    s_last = s_hist[-1]
    y_last = y_hist[-1]
    yy_last = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
    if yy_last > 1e-30:
        gamma_use = max(1e-8, float(np.dot(s_last, y_last)) / yy_last)
    else:
        gamma_use = 1.0

    r = gamma_use * q
    for i in range(m):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            continue
        beta_i = float(np.dot(s_i, y_i)) / yy
        r = r + (alphas[i] - beta_i) * y_i

    direction = -r
    if float(np.dot(g, direction)) > 0.0:
        direction = -g
    return direction


def spectral_partial(a, N, n, S, rng, frac):
    """
    Partially randomize Fourier phases, project back to simplex.
    A global escape mechanism that preserves a lot of spectral content.
    """
    frac = float(frac)
    if frac <= 0.0:
        return np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if frac >= 1.0:
        num = int(N)
    else:
        num = max(1, int(N * frac))
    num = min(num, int(N))

    A = fft(a, n=N)
    mag = np.abs(A)
    phase = np.angle(A)
    idx = rng.choice(N, size=num, replace=False)
    phase[idx] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, num)
    an = np.real(ifft(mag * np.exp(1j * phase)))[:n]
    an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
    return project_simplex(an, S)


def optimize_adam(a0, n, S, N, deadline, rng,
                  beta_start=4.0, beta_end=500.0):
    """
    Fast Adam-like LSE optimizer on the simplex.
    Used for robust warm-starting and escaping.
    """
    a = project_simplex(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), S)
    c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        a = np.full(n, float(S) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
        cmax = float(c.max())

    best_a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_c = cmax

    beta = float(beta_start)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-12

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    norm_a = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
    step_base = 0.10 * float(S) / float(n)
    if step_base <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(step_base):
        step_base = 1e-4

    last_best = time.monotonic()
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        if it % 100 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.2)

        if now - last_best > 60.0:
            step_base = max(1e-7 * float(S) / float(n), step_base * 0.7)
            if it % 2500 == 0:
                a = spectral_partial(a, N, n, S, rng, 0.05)
                c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
                cmax = float(c.max())
                m *= 0.5
                v *= 0.5
                last_best = time.monotonic()

        g, _ = lse_gradient(a, N, n, c, A, beta)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)

        mhat = m / (1.0 - (b1 ** t))
        vhat = v / (1.0 - (b2 ** t))
        step = step_base * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)

        snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
        cap = 0.05 * float(np.linalg.norm(a))
        if snorm > cap > 0.0:
            step *= cap / snorm

        cand = project_simplex(a - step, S)
        c_new, A_new = compute_conv(cand, N, n)
        cm = float(c_new.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            cm = float("inf")

        if cm > cmax + 1e-9:
            cand2 = project_simplex(a - 0.5 * step, S)
            c2, A2 = compute_conv(cand2, N, n)
            cm2 = float(c2.max())
            if not np.isfinite(cm2):
                cm2 = float("inf")
            if cm2 < cm:
                cand = cand2
                c_new, A_new, cm = c2, A2, cm2

            if cm > cmax + max(1e-8, 0.02 * max(1.0, cmax)):
                step_base = max(1e-7 * float(S) / float(n), step_base * 0.7)
                continue

        a = cand
        c = c_new
        A = A_new
        cmax = cm

        if cmax < best_c - 1e-10:
            best_a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
            best_c = cmax
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            step_base = min(0.30 * float(S) / float(n), step_base * 1.15)
        elif now - last_best > 120.0 and cmax > best_c + 0.05:
            a = np.array(best_a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
            c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
            cmax = float(c.max())
            m *= 0.5
            v *= 0.5

            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

    return project_simplex(best_a, S), best_c


def optimize_lbfgs(a0, n, S, N, deadline, rng,
                   beta_start=15.0, beta_end=1000.0, m_hist=12):
    """
    L-BFGS on LSE-smoothed max-convolution, with line search and restarts.
    """
    a = project_simplex(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), S)
    c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        a = np.full(n, float(S) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
        cmax = float(c.max())

    best_a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_c = cmax

    beta = float(beta_start)
    if beta > beta_end:
        beta = float(beta_end)

    s_hist = []
    y_hist = []

    norm_a = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
    step = 0.05 * norm_a if norm_a > 0.0 else 0.01 * float(S) / float(n)
    if step <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(step):
        step = 0.01 * float(S) / float(n)

    g, _ = lse_gradient(a, N, n, c, A, beta)
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        if it % 250 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.12)

        if not np.isfinite(cmax):
            break

        if now - last_best > 180.0:
            if cmax > best_c + 0.02:
                a = np.array(best_a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
            else:
                a = spectral_partial(a, N, n, S, rng, 0.18)
            c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
            cmax = float(c.max())
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []
            beta = max(float(beta_start), beta * 0.7)
            norm_a = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
            step = 0.05 * norm_a if norm_a > 0.0 else 0.01 * float(S) / float(n)
            g, _ = lse_gradient(a, N, n, c, A, beta)
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            continue

        direction = lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g, n)
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
        if dn < 1e-14:
            break
        direction = direction / dn

        a_old = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
        cmax_old = cmax

        # Candidate with current step.
        a1 = project_simplex(a_old + 1.0 * step * direction, S)
        c1, A1 = compute_conv(a1, N, n)
        cm1 = float(c1.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cm1):
            cm1 = float("inf")

        # Candidate with half step.
        a2 = project_simplex(a_old + 0.5 * step * direction, S)
        c2, A2 = compute_conv(a2, N, n)
        cm2 = float(c2.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cm2):
            cm2 = float("inf")

        # Candidate with double step (allows progress when step is too small).
        a3 = project_simplex(a_old + 2.0 * step * direction, S)
        c3, A3 = compute_conv(a3, N, n)
        cm3 = float(c3.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cm3):
            cm3 = float("inf")

        best_idx = 1
        best_cm = cm1
        best_mult = 1.0
        if cm2 < best_cm:
            best_idx = 2
            best_cm = cm2
            best_mult = 0.5
        if cm3 < best_cm:
            best_idx = 3
            best_cm = cm3
            best_mult = 2.0

        if best_cm <= cmax_old + 1e-8:
            if best_idx == 1:
                a_new, c_new, A_new = a1, c1, A1
            elif best_idx == 2:
                a_new, c_new, A_new = a2, c2, A2
            else:
                a_new, c_new, A_new = a3, c3, A3

            cmax = best_cm
            if cmax < best_c - 1e-10:
                best_a = np.array(a_new, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
                best_c = cmax
                last_best = time.monotonic()

            norm_new = float(np.linalg.norm(a_new))
            if best_mult >= 1.5:
                step = min(0.10 * norm_new, step * 1.20)
            elif best_mult <= 0.5:
                step = max(1e-8 * float(S) / float(n), step * 0.70)
            else:
                step = min(0.10 * norm_new, max(1e-8 * float(S) / float(n), step * 1.05))

            g_new, _ = lse_gradient(a_new, N, n, c_new, A_new, beta)
            s_k = a_new - a_old
            y_k = g_new - g
            sy = float(np.dot(s_k, y_k))
            ss = float(np.dot(s_k, s_k))
            if sy > 1e-12 * ss and ss > 1e-20:
                s_hist.append(s_k)
                y_hist.append(y_k)
                if len(s_hist) > m_hist:
                    s_hist = s_hist[-m_hist:]
                    y_hist = y_hist[-m_hist:]

            a, c, A, g = a_new, c_new, A_new, g_new
        else:
            step = max(1e-8 * float(S) / float(n), step * 0.6)
            if step < 1e-5 * float(S) / float(n):
                a = spectral_partial(a, N, n, S, rng, 0.06)
                c, A = compute_conv(a, N, n)
                cmax = float(c.max())
                s_hist = []
                y_hist = []
                beta = max(float(beta_start), beta * 0.8)
                g, _ = lse_gradient(a, N, n, c, A, beta)

    return project_simplex(best_a, S), best_c


def polish_candidate(a, n, S, N, deadline, rng,
                     adam_beta_start=4.0, adam_beta_end=500.0,
                     lbfgs_beta_start=15.0, lbfgs_beta_end=1000.0,
                     adam_frac=0.35):
    """Adam warm-up plus L-BFGS polish within a deadline."""
    a = project_simplex(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), S)
    cmax0 = conv_max(a, n, N)

    best_a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_c = cmax0

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline > now + 25.0:
        dur = min(adam_frac * (deadline - now), deadline - now - 5.0)
        if dur > 20.0:
            a2, s2 = optimize_adam(
                best_a, n, S, N,
                deadline=now + dur,
                rng=rng,
                beta_start=adam_beta_start,
                beta_end=adam_beta_end
            )
            if s2 < best_c:
                best_a, best_c = a2, s2

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline > now + 30.0:
        dur = min(0.85 * (deadline - now), deadline - now - 5.0)
        if dur > 25.0:
            a3, s3 = optimize_lbfgs(
                best_a, n, S, N,
                deadline=now + dur,
                rng=rng,
                beta_start=lbfgs_beta_start,
                beta_end=lbfgs_beta_end,
                m_hist=12
            )
            if s3 < best_c:
                best_a, best_c = a3, s3

    return project_simplex(best_a, S), best_c


def resize_seq(p, target):
    """
    Density-preserving resize of a probability vector p.
    p is internally normalized to sum 1 before returning.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    target = int(target)
    if target <= 0:
        return p
    m = int(p.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64) / float(target)

    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64) / float(target)
    p = p / s

    if m == target:
        return p.copy()

    x = np.arange(target, dtype=np.float64) * (float(m) / float(target))
    j = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
    j = np.clip(j, 0, m - 1)
    j2 = np.clip(j + 1, 0, m - 1)
    frac = x - j
    val = (1.0 - frac) * p[j] + frac * p[j2]
    val = val * (float(m) / float(target))
    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
    s2 = float(val.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64) / float(target)
    return val / s2


def make_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)


def make_u_shape(n, gamma):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    return np.maximum(1.0 + float(gamma) * t * t, 1e-6)


def make_beta_shape(n, a):
    i = np.arange(1.0, float(n) + 1.0)
    j = float(n) + 1.0 - i
    w = np.power(i, float(a)) * np.power(j, float(a))
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 1.0)
    return np.maximum(w, 1e-6)


def make_cos_shape(n, freq, amp, ph):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    w = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(ph))
    return np.maximum(w, 1e-6)


def make_random_uniform(n, rng):
    return rng.random(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-3


def make_spectral_random(n, N, rng, sigma=0.35):
    mag = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), N))
    ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, N)
    A = mag * np.exp(1j * ph)
    A[0] = float(n)
    a = np.real(ifft(A))[:n]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return make_random_uniform(n, rng)
    return a


def sort_cands_small(cands):
    """Sort list of [array, score] by score ascending."""
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        min_idx = i
        for j in range(i + 1, len(cands)):
            if cands[j][1] < cands[min_idx][1]:
                min_idx = j
        if min_idx != i:
            tmp = cands[i]
            cands[i] = cands[min_idx]
            cands[min_idx] = tmp
    return cands


def add_coarse_candidate(cands, arr, score, max_cands=3, corr_thr=0.985):
    """Add a coarse candidate if it is distinct enough and competitive."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return cands

    x = arr - float(np.mean(arr))
    nx = float(np.linalg.norm(x))
    for existing in cands:
        y = existing[0] - float(np.mean(existing[0]))
        ny = float(np.linalg.norm(y))
        if nx > 1e-14 and ny > 1e-14:
            corr = abs(float(np.dot(x, y)) / (nx * ny))
            if corr > corr_thr:
                return cands

    cands.append([np.array(arr, dtype=np.float64, copy=True), score])
    sort_cands_small(cands)
    if len(cands) > max_cands:
        cands.pop()
    return cands


def coarse_search(budget, start_time, rng, parent_seq):
    """
    Run many independent small-length searches.
    Returns up to 3 coarse probability arrays (sum 1).
    """
    m = 1024
    S_c = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(m))
    N_c = next_pow2(2 * m - 1)
    overall_deadline = start_time + max(1.0, float(budget))

    inits = []
    inits.append(make_uniform(m))
    inits.append(make_u_shape(m, 0.75))
    inits.append(make_u_shape(m, 1.75))
    inits.append(make_beta_shape(m, -0.18))
    inits.append(make_beta_shape(m, -0.30))
    inits.append(make_cos_shape(m, 1.0, 0.45, float(rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)))
    inits.append(make_cos_shape(m, 3.0, 0.40, float(rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)))
    inits.append(make_spectral_random(m, N_c, rng, 0.45))
    inits.append(make_random_uniform(m, rng))

    if parent_seq is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(parent_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
            par = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
            if par.size >= 32 and par.sum() > 0.0:
                inits.append(resize_seq(par, m))
        except Exception:
            pass

    cands = []
    total_inits = len(inits)

    for idx, init in enumerate(inits):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= overall_deadline - 5.0:
            break

        remaining = overall_deadline - now
        per = 0.88 * remaining / float(max(1, total_inits - idx))
        per = min(per, 160.0)
        block_deadline = now + per
        if block_deadline > overall_deadline:
            block_deadline = overall_deadline

        a = project_simplex(init * S_c, S_c)
        a, sc = polish_candidate(
            a, m, S_c, N_c,
            deadline=block_deadline,
            rng=rng,
            adam_beta_start=3.0,
            adam_beta_end=250.0,
            lbfgs_beta_start=10.0,
            lbfgs_beta_end=500.0,
            adam_frac=0.35
        )
        sc = conv_max(a, m, N_c)
        a_norm = a / a.sum() if float(a.sum()) > 0.0 else None
        if a_norm is not None:
            cands = add_coarse_candidate(cands, a_norm, sc, max_cands=3)

    sort_cands_small(cands)
    return cands


def improve_by_shifts(a, n, S, N, deadline, rng):
    """
    Final post-processing: symmetrize and search cyclic shifts.
    Cyclic shifts can change linear-convolution peaks and improve the score.
    """
    a = project_simplex(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1), S)
    best = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best_c = conv_max(best, n, N)

    # Symmetrization.
    sym = 0.5 * (best + best[::-1])
    sym = project_simplex(sym, S)
    sym_c = conv_max(sym, n, N)
    if sym_c < best_c - 1e-12:
        best = sym.copy()
        best_c = sym_c

    # A few deterministic shifts.
    for s in (0, n // 4, n // 2, 3 * n // 4, 1, n - 1):
        if int(s) < 0 or int(s) >= n:
            continue
        b = np.roll(best, int(s))
        bc = conv_max(b, n, N)
        if bc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best = b.copy()
            best_c = bc

    # Random shifts until the deadline.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if s == 0:
            continue
        b = np.roll(best, s)
        bc = conv_max(b, n, N)
        if bc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best = b.copy()
            best_c = bc

    return project_simplex(best, S), best_c


def propose_candidate():
    """
    Main search driver.
    Returns a list of non-negative floats minimizing the evaluator.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 30.0)
    total_budget = max(1.0, deadline - t0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20260217)

    n_full = 16384
    S_full = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_full))
    N_full = next_pow2(2 * n_full - 1)

    parent_np = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        if arr.size >= 64 and arr.sum() > 0.0:
            if arr.size > 20000:
                arr = resize_seq(arr, n_full)
            parent_np = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_np = None

    # Native parent candidate (same length as provided).
    native_a = None
    native_n = 0
    S_native = 0.0
    N_native = 0
    native_score = float("inf")

    if parent_np is not None and parent_np.size >= 256:
        native_n = int(parent_np.size)
        S_native = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(native_n))
        N_native = next_pow2(2 * native_n - 1)
        native_p = resize_seq(parent_np, native_n)
        native_a = project_simplex(native_p * S_native, S_native)
        native_score = conv_max(native_a, native_n, N_native)
        print("Native parent length=%d initial peak=%.8f" % (native_n, native_score))

    # Full-length working candidate.
    if parent_np is not None:
        full_base = resize_seq(parent_np, n_full)
    else:
        full_base = np.ones(n_full, dtype=np.float64)
    full_a = project_simplex(full_base * S_full, S_full)
    full_score = conv_max(full_a, n_full, N_full)
    print("Full length=%d initial peak=%.8f" % (n_full, full_score))

    # Phase 1: improve native parent if present.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if native_a is not None:
        dur = min(0.10 * total_budget, max(0.0, deadline - now - 420.0))
        if dur > 60.0:
            a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                native_a, native_n, S_native, N_native,
                deadline=now + dur,
                rng=rng,
                adam_beta_start=3.0,
                adam_beta_end=300.0,
                lbfgs_beta_start=15.0,
                lbfgs_beta_end=900.0
            )
            if s2 < native_score:
                native_a, native_score = a2, s2
            print("After native polish: peak=%.8f" % native_score)

            # If native improved, also update the full-length seeding.
            if native_score < full_score + 0.75:
                fa = project_simplex(resize_seq(native_a, n_full) * S_full, S_full)
                fs = conv_max(fa, n_full, N_full)
                if fs < full_score:
                    full_a, full_score = fa, fs

    # Phase 2: long polish of full-length candidate.
    now = time.monotonic()
    dur = min(0.22 * total_budget, max(0.0, deadline - now - 420.0))
    if dur > 90.0:
        a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
            full_a, n_full, S_full, N_full,
            deadline=now + dur,
            rng=rng,
            adam_beta_start=5.0,
            adam_beta_end=400.0,
            lbfgs_beta_start=20.0,
            lbfgs_beta_end=1200.0
        )
        if s2 < full_score:
            full_a, full_score = a2, s2
        print("After full initial polish: peak=%.8f" % full_score)

    # Phase 3: coarse global search.
    now = time.monotonic()
    coarse_top = []
    durc = min(0.10 * total_budget, max(0.0, deadline - now - 500.0))
    if durc > 120.0:
        coarse_top = coarse_search(durc, now, rng, parent_np)
        if coarse_top:
            print("Coarse best peak=%.8f" % coarse_top[0][1])

    # Phase 4: upsample top coarse candidates and Fourier-polish.
    if coarse_top:
        now = time.monotonic()
        durt = min(0.18 * total_budget, max(0.0, deadline - now - 360.0))
        if durt > 120.0:
            n_cand = min(2, len(coarse_top))
            per_time = durt / float(n_cand)
            for ci in range(n_cand):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if deadline - now < 80.0:
                    break
                block_time = min(per_time, max(0.0, deadline - now - 50.0))
                if block_time < 60.0:
                    break

                base_p = resize_seq(coarse_top[ci][0], n_full)
                fa = project_simplex(base_p * S_full, S_full)
                fs0 = conv_max(fa, n_full, N_full)

                a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                    fa, n_full, S_full, N_full,
                    deadline=now + block_time,
                    rng=rng,
                    adam_beta_start=5.0,
                    adam_beta_end=450.0,
                    lbfgs_beta_start=25.0,
                    lbfgs_beta_end=1500.0
                )
                if s2 < full_score:
                    full_a, full_score = a2, s2
                print("Coarse full candidate %d: upsample=%0.8f polished=%0.8f" %
                      (ci, fs0, s2))

    # Phase 5: adaptively polish whichever candidate is currently better.
    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now
        if rem < 220.0:
            break

        if native_a is not None and native_score <= full_score:
            work_native = True
        else:
            work_native = False

        dur = min(120.0, 0.25 * total_budget, rem - 100.0)
        if dur < 60.0:
            break

        slice_start = time.monotonic()
        if work_native:
            a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                native_a, native_n, S_native, N_native,
                deadline=slice_start + dur,
                rng=rng,
                adam_beta_start=10.0,
                adam_beta_end=600.0,
                lbfgs_beta_start=30.0,
                lbfgs_beta_end=1600.0
            )
            if s2 < native_score:
                native_a, native_score = a2, s2
        else:
            a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                full_a, n_full, S_full, N_full,
                deadline=slice_start + dur,
                rng=rng,
                adam_beta_start=10.0,
                adam_beta_end=600.0,
                lbfgs_beta_start=30.0,
                lbfgs_beta_end=1600.0
            )
            if s2 < full_score:
                full_a, full_score = a2, s2

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - slice_start
        if elapsed < 5.0:
            break

        # If the two lengths are close in quality, balance them in later time.
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now
        if rem < 220.0:
            break
        if native_a is not None and abs(native_score - full_score) < 0.008:
            if work_native:
                dur2 = min(90.0, 0.18 * total_budget, rem - 100.0)
                if dur2 > 50.0:
                    a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                        full_a, n_full, S_full, N_full,
                        deadline=now + dur2,
                        rng=rng,
                        adam_beta_start=10.0,
                        adam_beta_end=600.0,
                        lbfgs_beta_start=30.0,
                        lbfgs_beta_end=1600.0
                    )
                    if s2 < full_score:
                        full_a, full_score = a2, s2
            else:
                dur2 = min(90.0, 0.18 * total_budget, rem - 100.0)
                if dur2 > 50.0:
                    a2, s2 = polish_candidate(
                        native_a, native_n, S_native, N_native,
                        deadline=now + dur2,
                        rng=rng,
                        adam_beta_start=10.0,
                        adam_beta_end=600.0,
                        lbfgs_beta_start=30.0,
                        lbfgs_beta_end=1600.0
                    )
                    if s2 < native_score:
                        native_a, native_score = a2, s2

    # Final shift/symmetrization on both length classes if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if native_a is not None and deadline - now > 25.0:
        d = min(18.0, max(1.0, deadline - now - 5.0))
        native_a, native_score = improve_by_shifts(
            native_a, native_n, S_native, N_native,
            deadline=now + d,
            rng=rng
        )

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline - now > 25.0:
        d = min(18.0, max(1.0, deadline - now - 5.0))
        full_a, full_score = improve_by_shifts(
            full_a, n_full, S_full, N_full,
            deadline=now + d,
            rng=rng
        )

    print("Final native peak=%.8f, full peak=%.8f" % (native_score, full_score))

    # Choose best sequence.
    if native_a is not None and native_score <= full_score:
        best_a = native_a
    else:
        best_a = full_a

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    blen = int(best_a.size)
    if blen < 32:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        blen = 1024

    S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(blen))
    best_a = project_simplex(best_a, S_best)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    best_a = project_simplex(best_a, S_best)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * 1024.0)
        best_a = project_simplex(best_a, S_best)

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
